# Independent validation log

This is the chronological record of the independent verifier of the campaign, a separate implementation written from the exchange-format description and the pre-registered specification. All entries are dated in UTC. References to evidence records name files of the full validation archive, which is not part of the published folder because of its size. Sections 6 and 9 of the accompanying report summarize the confirmed results.

## Current verified state, 2026-10-29T16:40:23.920784+00:00

The main outcome is C: an unsuccessful numerical search, not a nonexistence result. The separate D result applies to uniformly decaying Cartesian C2 axisymmetric profiles with 0 < c_l < 1/2. General smooth existence at c_l = 1/2 and above one half remains OPEN; the critical real analytic subclass is excluded. The 21-group main campaign-report audit and later deltas are indexed in the validation archive. Latest report delta 6, SHA256 8ec6d3f1bd6a9d4c42a7c35f156b2c76eab7a104d34a46cf7cf05d4bd5428c1e, passes nine checks in the validation archive. Its 15:43:51Z assembly passes all 14 transport groups in the validation archive. The report author has withdrawn the proposed post-stop report edits, so the audited bytes are intended to remain final. This does not pre-certify a future rebuild. The origin v11, pressure v9 and obstruction v39 supporting-document changes have separate confirmed audits.

All 21 primary exports have full registered evaluations and reject A1/A2. The 13:31:03Z coverage inventory passed 412 checks, adding only the two completed c_l = 0.745 follow-up suites. Both protected portable R5 reproductions and the native interruption replay reproduced rejection. The latest portable test used isolated NumPy 2.5.0, SciPy 1.18.0 and mpmath 1.3.0 and completed at 13:22:44Z. An archived file (in the validation archive) selects the actual test and audit records. Full A7 remains unestablished. The ten nested fields retain their finite-regularity and failed-core-E limitations.

The last requested resource prefix contains 941 observations and 433 PID/start-tick identities through 2026-10-29T16:39:12.795298+00:00. The independently replayed maximum utime + stime counters sum to 79452.12 CPU seconds, including 48.76 seconds of observer bookkeeping. A conservative statement is at least 79452 CPU seconds by that actual last sample. The separate bracketed-window increase is 62416.23 seconds; it overlaps and must not be added. Earlier jobs, unsampled short processes and later counter increases remain omitted. The result is an incomplete CPU lower bound, not an exact campaign total or GPU kernel time. The largest observed individual RSS is 594161664 bytes, an observed maximum rather than a certified peak. The collector actually started at 2026-10-29T16:40:00.000107+00:00, completed at 2026-10-29T16:40:00.130685+00:00, and used 0.126770 measured wrapper seconds. All 14 independent resource checks pass in the validation archive; the frozen prefix and audit are in the validation archive. The main report deliberately keeps its earlier, separately confirmed lower bound of 79443 CPU seconds by 15:29:08Z. These overlapping snapshots must not be added. Final file assembly after the last sample is not included in this lower bound.

Earlier OPEN states, superseded expectations and corrected claims remain in chronological records below. Dated reviews apply only to their named snapshots. The 16:44:44Z team stop is scheduled, not yet elapsed. The two primary-search status qualifications requested at 15:26:27Z were resolved and independently confirmed at 15:35:00Z in the validation archive. All 388 primary payload hashes pass; only the primary search's work log and status log changed. The corrected manifest is 30e6d60117a532fef66ba18b9f3780dc0b36dd4628c0b5ae2eb98859650b2432. The 15:43:51Z delta 6 assembly transport is confirmed. The last requested resource sample and independent replay are complete. The final artifact build and check follow this current-document refresh; their dated receipt remains separate.

The initial direct-Newton axial quadrature failure remains in `operator_tests_initial.json`. It was repaired by analytic pole subtraction. The first far-field radius test, also retained, was extended to R=1024 because the alpha=1 pressure correction converges slowly. No test tolerance was relaxed to obtain a pass.

The one-command validator was executed on `MANUFACTURED_operator_fixture.json`, SHA256 `550af1560a6509dbf063b7a3038c40999170322133491191e9feecb2b07779f1`. It measured order-one reduced defects and a worst primitive relative RMS about 0.419 using the four-term diagnostic denominator (the preserved earlier three-term result was about 0.434), as expected for a non-solution. Its successful execution is a workflow test, not a candidate result.

## 2026-10-28T23:16:45.934650+00:00: generalized rational T1 family has a missing forced axis power

New the supplement extends the fixed-b=3 result. For any a>0, real b and 1/2<c_l<1, phi=z(1+b z^2)/(2(1+a s+z^2)^(p+1)) cannot be an exact smooth Euler profile with K=q(0,0) nonzero. The cubic source relation forces b>p+1>0. On the outgoing axis, h=H z^(-2p)(1-b z^(-2p)+o(z^(-2p))) and w eta=2K^2 h(h-1), so eta has a strictly positive z^(-1-4p) coefficient (2K^2 H/c_l)[H+b+b/c_l]. Geometric -Delta_5 phi has only z^(-1-2p) followed by z^(-3-2p); since p<1 it cannot supply the intervening coefficient. Sixteen exact symbolic checks passed in 0.2590592158958316 seconds. Verdict: CONFIRMED by the standalone argument, pending report-author cross-review. It excludes the exact shape, not deformations, and does not give a positive residual floor. This is relevant to the report author's t1scal question: even an intersection of its three scalar conditions cannot close the exact b-family.

## 2026-10-29T01:05:50.327907+00:00: quantitative criterion for the characteristic swirl comparison

Method: wrote the standalone proof in the validation archive, then verified its signs and constants on nonlinear logistic and anisotropic source fixtures. If the backward flow obeys |X_t(x)-x_*| <= C exp(-lambda t)|x-x_*| and a=1-2phi_z vanishes at the source with a Lipschitz bound, the normalized swirl is q_K=K exp(-integral_0^infinity a(X_t)dt). The error of another source-normalized q is the weighted characteristic integral of R_q. A source-weighted residual supremum gives a C0 error bound; controlling the derivative of R_q and the flow derivative gives a C1 bound, which in turn bounds partial_z(q^2-q_K^2).

Result: all 94 checks passed at 2026-10-29T01:05:13.682293+00:00 in 0.994746551848948 seconds wall time and 0.985059003 CPU seconds. The checks include the oriented interface-jump sign and independent quadrature of both the error and its derivative. Proof SHA256 is e0b30ceae6ad89864a9fdf1c29b2caf32952d35ad345a42727d3b5734f594096. CPU48 was paused for 2.30274128774181 seconds and resumed.

Verdict: CONFIRMED the conditional criterion. Its hypotheses have not been certified for L1 or L2: they include a global backward contraction bound on the domain, exact source compatibility, a residual derivative bound, and appropriate interface terms. Application to the saved fields remains OPEN. This explains precisely what source-adaptive samples can diagnose and what they do not prove.

For report-author cross-review: please check the integrating-factor sign, the 1/(2 lambda^2) term in the derivative bound, and the stated limitations for nonzero a0 and piecewise jumps. Read locally only; no JavaScript/TypeScript, servers, RLScience/cluster, or remote/paid compute.

SPEC9.1 distinctions: the reported q0=3.5 sequence 2.55/2.33/2.83 satisfies the numeric >0.7 successive-ratio part of the plateau rule and the D36/48/64 growth is at least 1.3. It does not satisfy the separate stall rule's1.4 growth. A formal floor additionally requires the best B2-admissible holdout value per level, so replacing a1.88 D36 result with its later 2.55 continuation does not define that floor. The q0=2.5 sequence 4.24/2.79/3.37 fails the first 0.7 ratio test, and its D64 dominant equation is q in section 15, not axial momentum. F7 also requires that F1-F6 are not dominant; measured interface failures and source defects must be considered. The q0 scan rises in momentum from 0.91 to 1.40 at q0=5 to 6; 'keeps falling' and 'within noise' lack support.

The live lm.py changed again during review: it now uses truncated SVD (defaulttrunc_rel=1e-4), rather than the earlier Tikhonov variant. Its phi derivative -N diag(1/cs) V_k S_k^-1 U_k^T G does not generally differentiate the retained singular subspace. Even with no truncation, a nonzero inner residual contributes another normal-equation derivative term. A small relative error in total random Jv can hide a substantial error in the q-mediated part. The claim that gradient and Gauss-Newton model are correct needs targeted checks along the gradient, rejected steps and q-sensitive directions, multiple finite-difference sizes, and separate Dq comparisons. I am writing a standalone proof and small independent counterexamples. Exact numerical effect on the Euler run remains OPEN. Frozen sources: recorded in the validation archive.

For report-author review: please reconcile these points before adopting the section 17 diagnosis. Local files only; no JavaScript/TypeScript, servers, RLScience/cluster or remote/paid compute.

## 2026-10-29T03:09:37.881727+00:00: global transport regularity argument for the canonical fields

Method: new the supplement proves that a smooth vector field with W dot x >= gamma |x|^2, at most linear growth and a positive diagonal source linearization has a unique smooth normalized h = exp(integral a(Phi_t x) dt) when a(0)=0. The backward flow and all its positive-order derivatives decay exponentially on compact sets; this justifies differentiating the integral at every finite order. The damped resolvent gives a unique bounded-source smooth e for (1+c_l+W dot grad)e=partial_z(h^2). Reflection and rotation commute with the construction. For the canonical two-series fields, h=1+O(R^2), e=O(R), and the parity follows exactly.

Result: the seven canonical coefficient files have exactly P1(0,0)+P2(0,0)=1/2 in rational arithmetic and positive source rates. These seven checks and 51 elementary linear-flow integral checks passed at 2026-10-29T03:09:17.627841Z. The manufactured integrals test the normalization and resolvent factors; they do not certify the nonlinear proof. If the proof is accepted, the supplement's finite-ball smoothness hypothesis is discharged for these seven canonical potentials, not for the piecewise ladder fields. It supplies no interval enclosure or elliptic closure.

Verdict: CONFIRMED the exact input normalization and the manufactured checks. OPEN independent cross-review of the new smoothness argument. For the report author: please check the uniform compact-set derivative bound for the backward flow, the two integral formulas and their scope before using the stronger conclusion. No change is made to the already hashed the supplement proof or result files.

## 2026-10-29T04:57:43.024520+00:00: Rm5 pressure, flux, Cartesian and adversarial checks complete

The immutable input is in the validation archive, SHA256 12dfdcbfd44247669d19a0c0ab7e81bfa22ec0dcb6abd052e0633aa682d9830b. All results below use this input; no optimization or coefficient change is involved.

1. **Full-space pressure. Method:** independent bulk tr(DU^2) reconstruction, spherical Green orders 32/40 and then 64/80, direct Newton quadrature at 20 points, and raw origin moments at three quadrature levels through Green degree 96. **Result:** degree-40 pressure differs from direct Newton by 3.78124e-6; the refined degree-80 difference is 6.77179692876e-9. Orders 64 to 80 change pressure by at most 5.11102271616e-12 and its gradient by 3.10240722001e-12 at these points. Direct finite-difference gradients at five points differ from the refined gradient by at most 5.97187370666e-8; this larger value is explicitly retained. The separately computed stress surface correction has maximum pressure 2.49077829045e-6 and gradient 2.31207265038e-5 on the original 20-point set. **Verdict: CONFIRMED** the stated finite-point comparisons; **REFUTED** a blanket claim that the degree-40 calculation already agreed to 1e-8; **OPEN** the full normalized A7 test and any global error enclosure. The bulk pressure of a piecewise field is not a repair of its interface failure.

2. **Origin defect. Method:** direct raw source moments with mapped infinite tail and independent Green moments, plus 12 origin Gauss/collar sets with primary and independently detected core radii. **Result:** P(0) is approximately -2.20989507723; partial_z P(0) is 0.159480436670; the pressure-free axial residual is -0.115779687021. The full axial residual is 0.0437007496489 and changes by less than 6.8e-14 across the three direct moment calculations. The independently detected origin-collar rho values at degrees 32/80/96 are 1.79388955585289, 1.79388955585424, 1.79388955585225. **Verdict: CONFIRMED** persistence of the dominant registered defect under this pressure refinement. The regular polynomial core does not satisfy the Euler equation at its origin; this is not an observed origin singularity.

3. **Energy and helicity. Method:** split radial volume quadrature, 12x32 and 24x64 rules, radii a_eff, 4a_eff and 16a_eff in working coordinates, degree-32 bulk pressure, independently oriented interface fluxes and residual work. **Result:** the fine relative raw energy defects are 0.155688040965, 0.0339515932176 and 0.00212479144522; corresponding helicity defects are 0.129526450657, 0.0806401974069 and 0.0169294668832. Adding the measured residual-work and interface terms gives energy identity relative errors at most 1.11e-11 and helicity errors at most 5.35e-13. **Verdict: REFUTED** satisfaction of A9; **CONFIRMED** the stated piecewise identity checks. ## 2026-10-29T07:41:21.359689+00:00: pressure bandwidth scope qualification

Method: a standalone informal argument followed by eight exact Cartesian symbolic checks. Evidence: recorded in the validation archive. The current the main report is frozen in the validation archive (SHA256 6cf8125065338b1d87f174f5b739472f6c97e91361bb0ec5102936eae4b7476f).

Result: the bound D = 2 max(N,M)+2 is sufficient, but it does not force nonzero source modes above degree 32 whenever max(N,M)>15. For the globally smooth, rapidly decaying pure swirl q=exp(-R^2), phi=0, the source has angular degree 2 and so does its Newton pressure, even in a representation permitting M=16. Even minimal local degree 16 does not force degree 34: q=z^16 has source -2z^32. All eight checks pass. Neither field is an Euler similarity profile.

Verdict: REFUTED for the universal omission sentence in main report section 3.7; CONFIRMED for the sufficient bandwidth theorem. Use: "A degree 32 truncation can therefore omit source modes when max(N,M)>15; actual omission depends on the nonzero source coefficients." A full-space pressure bandwidth conclusion also needs the bound uniformly on every radial piece, as stated in the original proof, rather than on just one piece. The local counterexample is not claimed to decay. The standalone qualification is ready for report-author cross-review through the report author; that review is OPEN.

## 2026-10-29T07:55:28.157251+00:00: exact attainment of source modes above degree 32

Method: the new standalone supplement in the validation archive derives the top angular coefficient of tr(DU^2), proves sharpness for every angular degree, and states the limits of the conclusion. Frozen binary coefficients are converted to exact rational JSON and checked back against all 14 archive hashes. There are 664 passing exact algebra, Cartesian differentiation, radial-basis and source-data checks in the validation archive.

Result: at raw R = 1 the corrected old L0, old L1, R5, reflection, both 0.68 seeds and the original/continued 0.74 D36 fields have exact source degree 50; old L2/L3sep and the two 0.74 D48 fields have degree 66; the 0.74 D64 export has degree 88. All 13 noncritical fields therefore have an exactly nonzero source mode above degree 32. Their Newton pressure cannot have all modes above 32 identically zero. The critical export has degree 26 at this radius.

Verdict: CONFIRMED for these exact finite-data calculations. This supports actual omission for these specified exports, while leaving the universal implication refuted by the earlier qualification. It gives no pressure-error lower bound: the top D48 coefficients here range from about 7e-21 to 2.5e-19, and D64 is about 9.7e-26. Pressure convergence and A7 remain separate. The general bound is sharp because phi = R^2 z^N gives top source coefficient (8N^2+40N+56)R^(2N+2), and q = R^2 z^M gives 4R^(2M+4); smooth cutoffs give globally decaying kinematic examples, not Euler profiles.

The proof and exact rational application are ready for report-author cross-review through the report author; that cross-review is OPEN. The first checker invocation failed only while assembling source-hash paths, was corrected and rerun, and both bounded pauses resumed the pressure worker.

Verdict: CONFIRMED for these independently reconstructed finite-grid comparisons. The wide-grid angular 32 fit reaches about 0.00706 against the frozen source, and about 0.007056 with the independent source. The saved angular 24 potential's0.0434 discrepancy is therefore not a lower bound on what the larger frozen-source trial space can represent. The direct core-only fits have smaller core E but enormous far-band errors, independently reproduced. Neither comparison proves an infimum, a nonlinear solution, global source portrait, exact affine-constraint satisfaction or any primitive Euler acceptance threshold. The saved fits' matrix rank and normal-gradient claims are still primary diagnostics, not independently certified here.

The standalone proof in the validation archive identifies precisely the gap: L phi-T(phi)=(L phi-eta_0)+(eta_0-T(phi)), while the fit evaluates only the first term. Self-closed E also changes the denominator. The report author's current conclusion is supported only in this discrete frozen-source sense; avoid presenting 0.007 as a resolution theorem for the nonlinear problem. Evidence: recorded in the validation archive and the retained archived file. Report-author cross-review can inspect the proof and these six independent comparisons as the continuing exchange.

## 2026-10-29T09:11:00.300449+00:00: exact repair and independent check of the frozen-source constraints

Method: a new standalone proof extracts the four elliptic axis-tail coefficient differences directly from the represented source and tail terms. Compact splines and the inherited seed cancel. Tail column 1 is Z(mu) = mu(1-mu^2); its pole value is zero and its derivative is -2. Hence each of the four tail envelopes changes its leading elliptic pole coefficient by -8 times its coefficient, with one triangular cross-term from the first envelope. Four explicit rational corrections restore T1, T2, U1 and T3 exactly to the angular-level 2a endpoint, while the source coefficient u_0 was already identical. The correction vanishes on the whole axis and near the origin. Each corrected field is retained as exact rational JSON. This repairs the frozen affine constraints, not the Euler equations.

Result: all 747 source-identity, exact-constraint and numerical comparison checks pass. The largest absolute coefficient correction over the six fits is 3.182645227321389e-09. Their fixed-source E values change by at most 8.673617379884035e-18 in floating-point evaluation. For the wide-window fits, the corrected independent-Laplacian errors against the primary frozen source are 0.0113999407231448, 0.0103826196123656 and 0.007062968814036441 for level 2a, level 2b and angular 32. Against the independently integrated endpoint source, the angular 32 value is 0.007055610126260888. Core-only fits retain their large far-band errors.

Verdict: CONFIRMED for exact satisfaction of these five frozen affine constraints and for the stated finite-grid comparisons. Their numerical E values have no interval enclosure. The endpoint U1 target is retained, not asserted to vanish exactly. No fit has been transported as its own source, and K, S and H were not recomputed; nonlinear closure and a rigorous discretization error bound remain OPEN. The smaller frozen-source error supports representability of this one frozen source on these grids. It does not prove that nonlinear closure cannot be limited by resolution, or that the current nonlinear iterate is stationary. Report-author review of this proof and the earlier refined-basis proof is requested through these team notes and remains OPEN.

Evidence: recorded in the validation archive; standalone proof in the validation archive (SHA256 92fcf9a001cf264226d55ac8376867db28773ef28c68e5691be7b70080b4510a). Actual computation 3.47875 wall seconds, 3.44162 CPU seconds. The paused D64 worker resumed successfully, recorded in the validation archive.

The omitted source order lies strictly below the fourth power. This closes the specific derivation gap in its note independently.

## 2026-10-29T09:35:33.977468+00:00: t1nk5 interim iteration counts and conditional spectral scope

Method: freeze the primary history, summary, mathematical proof and finite spectrum records, then independently count accepted iterations and recompute best and last E from the histories. The 12 arithmetic checks pass. Evidence: recorded in the validation archive. Primary numerical code was read but not executed.

Result: ordinary Picard accepted one damped half step in two iterations, taking E from 0.0391480051881 to 1.65349263861. The Anderson experiment accepted 30 fallback damped Picard steps and zero accelerated Anderson steps; its last E is 0.184691627642 and its best is still the initial 0.0391480051881. Thus that note's unqualified statement that Picard and Anderson never accepted a step is REFUTED. The precise statement is that neither experiment improved the best E and no accelerated Anderson step was accepted. A scalar-root failure is a failure of the tested completion procedure, not proof that no other root exists.

The standalone primary closure proof correctly derives the changing-weight least-squares derivative, conditional on full rank and differentiable transport/completion. The exact implication from a real derivative eigenvalue lambda>1 to an eigenvalue 1+beta(lambda-1)>1 for every constant beta>0 is CONFIRMED. Its application to the true derivative remains conditional: the saved finite matrix and numerical eigenpairs are not certified enclosures of that derivative. The endpoint is not a proved fixed point. The source-change and elliptic-repair decomposition is correct, with the original shell denominators held fixed; E_new minus E_frozen is not the norm of the source change.

The full 1855-dimensional floating-point spectrum now reports 214 eigenvalues outside the unit circle, compared with 60 of 64 Arnoldi Ritz values. This is a numerical count for a stored finite matrix, not a certified count of modes of an exact map or the Euler problem. The report also gives large eigenvalue condition estimates for some near-unit modes. A small eigen-residual alone does not resolve that sensitivity. Independent checking of the stored matrix is pending. The relative far-axis mode peak is not the location of the largest unnormalized vorticity variation.

Verdict: CONFIRMED for the history arithmetic and conditional mathematical statements; REFUTED for the unqualified no-accepted-step wording; OPEN for a certified spectral conclusion about the exact nonlinear map. Report-author follow-up: please review these qualifications before incorporating the final 10:42Z record. No final t1nk5 endpoint is declared here.

## 2026-10-29T10:38:33.884333+00:00: companion-document review and resonant Green constant

Read the complete docrev answer. Finding24 is CONFIRMED by a new standalone supplement written before its 80 exact symbolic checks. In dimension d=3 or 5, put n=2ell+d-2. For exterior source a R^(-ell-d) and interior moment a log(R/Rstar)+Cstar, the exact inverse is R^(-ell-d+2)[(a/n)log(R/Rstar)+Cstar/n+a/n^2]. If only the forced logarithm is subtracted, the remaining homogeneous coefficient includes a/n^2. A different subtraction convention can absorb that constant, but must be stated. The existing independent reconstruction integrates both terms and is unaffected. The proof is indexed as a supplement to the pressure topic.

For the report author: include this supplement in the report-author cross-review and confirm the subtraction convention in the revised pressure document. The proof needs only the already checked Green formula; it makes no Euler existence claim.

One correction to docrev itself: its final provenance row repeats the now-refuted all-at-R10000 description. The actual D64 archive has 17 infinities atR2848.035868435799 and 17NaNs at each of R5336.699231206308 and R10000. The full finite mask and all numerical pressure comparisons were already correct. Use the 10:30 correction, not that historical row. Evidence: recorded in the validation archive.
